  // current row\n    const double* __restrict ryp; // row y+1 (clamped)\n    const double* __restrict ryn; // row y-1 (clamped)\n    const double* __restrict rzp; // row z+1 (clamped)\n    const double* __restrict rzn; // row z-1 (clamped)\n};\n\ninline RowPtrs makeRowPtrs(const double* c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                           const size_t y, const size_t z) noexcept {\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    return RowPtrs{c + idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny), c + idx3(0, yp, z, nx, ny), c + idx3(0, yn, z, nx, ny),\n                   c + idx3(0, y, zp, nx, ny), c + idx3(0, y, zn, nx, ny)};\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential for a single row (fixed y, z)\ninline void chemicalPotentialRow(const double* __restrict c, double* __restrict mu,\n                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                 const size_t y, const size_t z) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n    const RowPtrs p = makeRowPtrs(c, nx, ny, nz, y, z);\n    const double* __restrict r = p.r;\n    const double* __restrict ryp = p.ryp;\n    const double* __restrict ryn = p.ryn;\n    const double* __restrict rzp = p.rzp;\n    const double* __restrict rzn = p.rzn;\n    double* __restrict m = mu + base;\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    auto edge = [&](const size_t x) {\n        const double cv = r[x];\n        m[x] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n               + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n               - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n    };\n\n    edge(0);\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                mu[idx] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\n\n# validateResult parallel\nvs=src.index('bool validateResult'); i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n'''+src[ve:]\n\nold=src[src.index('    // Allocate arrays'):src.index('    auto end = std::chrono')]\nnew2=r'''    // Allocate arrays (uninitialized; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfgkGPUJBWsCeBWje2aLw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Qc2rQjFmap339iofoHMsD2","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/llmtest/evals/20261002-142720/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5.5-cc-medium_omp_r3/cahn-hilliard; python3 - <<'EOF'\np='cahn_hilliard.cpp'; s=open(p).read()\na=s.index('// Compute Laplacian with clamped'); b=s.index('bool validateResult')\nnew=r'''// Pointers to the five rows needed for a 7-point Laplacian of one row\n// (neighbors already clamped at the domain boundaries).\nstruct RowPtrs {\n    const double* r;   // current row\n    const double* ryp; // row y+1 (clamped)\n    const double* ryn; // row y-1 (clamped)\n    const double* rzp; // row z+1 (clamped)\n    const double* rzn; // row z-1 (clamped)\n};\n\n// Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions at position x of a row,\n// with xp/xn the (clamped) x neighbors.\ninline double laplacianAt(const RowPtrs& p, const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn,\n                          const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {\n    const double cxx = (p.r[xp] + p.r[xn] - 2.0 * p.r[x]) / dx2;\n    const double cyy = (p.ryp[x] + p.ryn[x] - 2.0 * p.r[x]) / dy2;\n    const double czz = (p.rzp[x] + p.rzn[x] - 2.0 * p.r[x]) / dz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential of a single row: m[x] = mu(x, y, z)\ninline void chemicalPotentialRow(const RowPtrs& p, double* __restrict m, const size_t nx,\n                                 const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) noexcept {\n    auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n        const double cv = p.r[x];\n        return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n               + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n               - gamma * laplacianAt(p, x, xp, xn, dx2, dy2, dz2);\n    };\n    m[0] = point(0, (nx > 1) ? 1 : 0, 0);\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n        m[x] = point(x, x + 1, x - 1);\n    }\n    if (nx > 1) m[nx - 1] = point(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2);\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update of a single row: cn[x] = co[x] + dt * D * lap(mu)(x, y, z)\ninline void cahnHilliardRow(const RowPtrs& p, double* __restrict cn, const double* __restrict co, const size_t nx,\n                            const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                            const double D, const double dt) noexcept {\n    auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n        return co[x] + dt * D * laplacianAt(p, x, xp, xn, dx2, dy2, dz2);\n    };\n    cn[0] = point(0, (nx > 1) ? 1 : 0, 0);\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n        cn[x] = point(x, x + 1, x - 1);\n    }\n    if (nx > 1) cn[nx - 1] = point(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2);\n}\n\n// Domain decomposition into tiles of (z-slab x y-block). Each tile is\n// processed by one thread, streaming through z while keeping a rolling\n// 3-plane window of the chemical potential (for the tile's y-rows plus halo)\n// in a small cache-resident buffer. This fuses the chemical-potential and\n// update kernels, so mu never has to round-trip through main memory.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;  // rows per y-block\n    size_t nyb;     // number of y-blocks\n    size_t zSlab;   // planes per z-slab\n    size_t nzs;     // number of z-slabs\n    size_t ntiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t nthreads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n    // Keep the 3-plane mu window (3 * (yBlock + 2) * nx doubles) around 256 KiB\n    const size_t rows = (256 * 1024) / (3 * sizeof(double) * std::max<size_t>(nx, 1));\n    size_t yb = (rows > 10) ? rows - 2 : 8;\n    yb = std::min(std::max<size_t>(yb, 1), std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    t.yBlock = (ny + t.nyb - 1) / t.nyb;  // balance block sizes\n    t.nyb = (ny + t.yBlock - 1) / t.yBlock;\n    // Enough z-slabs that every thread gets (at least) one tile\n    size_t nzs = (nthreads + t.nyb - 1) / t.nyb;\n    nzs = std::min(std::max<size_t>(nzs, 1), std::max<size_t>(nz, 1));\n    t.zSlab = (nz + nzs - 1) / nzs;\n    t.nzs = (nz + t.zSlab - 1) / t.zSlab;\n    t.ntiles = t.nzs * t.nyb;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// One full time step on one tile: reads cold, writes cnew.\n// muBuf must hold 3 * (yBlock + 2) * nx doubles.\nvoid processTile(const Tiling& tl, const size_t tile, double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n                 double* __restrict muBuf,\n                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                 const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t z0 = (tile / tl.nyb) * tl.zSlab;\n    const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + tl.zSlab, nz);\n    const size_t y0 = (tile % tl.nyb) * tl.yBlock;\n    const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + tl.yBlock, ny);\n    if (z0 >= z1 || y0 >= y1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n\n    // mu rows needed: [rlo, rhi], planes needed: [plo, phi] (inclusive, clamped)\n    const size_t rlo = (y0 > 0) ? y0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t rhi = (y1 < ny) ? y1 : ny - 1;\n    const size_t plo = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t winRows = rhi - rlo + 1;\n    const size_t slotSize = winRows * nx;\n\n    auto slot = [&](const size_t z) { return muBuf + (z % 3) * slotSize; };\n    auto row = [&](const double* base, const size_t y, const size_t z) { return base + z * plane + y * nx; };\n\n    size_t next = plo;  // next mu plane to compute\n    for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n\n        // Compute mu planes up to zp into the rolling window\n        for (; next <= zp; ++next) {\n            const size_t mz = next;\n            const size_t mzp = (mz < nz - 1) ? mz + 1 : mz;\n            const size_t mzn = (mz > 0) ? mz - 1 : 0;\n            double* dst = slot(mz);\n            for (size_t y = rlo; y <= rhi; ++y) {\n                const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n                const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n                const RowPtrs p{row(cold, y, mz), row(cold, yp, mz), row(cold, yn, mz),\n                                row(cold, y, mzp), row(cold, y, mzn)};\n                chemicalPotentialRow(p, dst + (y - rlo) * nx, nx, dx2, dy2, dz2, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update concentration for plane z, rows [y0, y1)\n        const double* mc = slot(z);\n        const double* mp = slot(zp);\n        const double* mn = slot(zn);\n        for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const RowPtrs p{mc + (y - rlo) * nx, mc + (yp - rlo) * nx, mc + (yn - rlo) * nx,\n                            mp + (y - rlo) * nx, mn + (y - rlo) * nx};\n            const size_t off = z * plane + y * nx;\n            cahnHilliardRow(p, cnew + off, cold + off, nx, dx2, dy2, dz2, D, dt);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field. Pages of both field buffers are first-touched\n// by the thread that will later process the corresponding tile (NUMA locality).\nvoid initializeConcentration(const Tiling& tl, double* c, double* cnew,\n                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tl.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / tl.nyb) * tl.zSlab;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + tl.zSlab, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % tl.nyb) * tl.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + tl.yBlock, ny);\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                    cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\na=s.index('    // Allocate arrays ('); 1 : 0, 0);\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n        m[x] = point(x, x + 1, x - 1);\n    }\n    if (nx > 1) m[nx - 1] = point(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2);\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update of a single row: cn[x] = co[x] + dt * D * lap(mu)(x, y, z)\ninline void cahnHilliardRow(const RowPtrs& p, double* __restrict cn, const double* __restrict co, const size_t nx,\n                            const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                            const double D, const double dt) noexcept {\n    auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n        return co[x] + dt * D * laplacianAt(p, x, xp, xn, dx2, dy2, dz2);\n    };\n    cn[0] = point(0, (nx > 1) ? 1 : 0, 0);\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n        cn[x] = point(x, x + 1, x - 1);\n    }\n    if (nx > 1) cn[nx - 1] = point(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2);\n}\n\n// Domain decomposition into tiles of (z-slab x y-block). Each tile is\n// processed by one thread, streaming through z while keeping a rolling\n// 3-plane window of the chemical potential (for the tile's y-rows plus halo)\n// in a small cache-resident buffer. This fuses the chemical-potential and\n// update kernels, so mu never has to round-trip through main memory.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;  // rows per y-block\n    size_t nyb;     // number of y-blocks\n    size_t zSlab;   // planes per z-slab\n    size_t nzs;     // number of z-slabs\n    size_t ntiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t nthreads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n    // Keep the 3-plane mu window (3 * (yBlock + 2) * nx doubles) around 256 KiB\n    const size_t rows = (256 * 1024) / (3 * sizeof(double) * std::max<size_t>(nx, 1));\n    size_t yb = (rows > 10) ? rows - 2 : 8;\n    yb = std::min(std::max<size_t>(yb, 1), std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    t.yBlock = (ny + t.nyb - 1) / t.nyb;  // balance block sizes\n    t.nyb = (ny + t.yBlock - 1) / t.yBlock;\n    // Enough z-slabs that every thread gets (at least) one tile\n    size_t nzs = (nthreads + t.nyb - 1) / t.nyb;\n    nzs = std::min(std::max<size_t>(nzs, 1), std::max<size_t>(nz, 1));\n    t.zSlab = (nz + nzs - 1) / nzs;\n    t.nzs = (nz + t.zSlab - 1) / t.zSlab;\n    t.ntiles = t.nzs * t.nyb;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// One full time step on one tile: reads cold, writes cnew.\n// muBuf must hold 3 * (yBlock + 2) * nx doubles.\nvoid processTile(const Tiling& tl, const size_t tile, double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n                 double* __restrict muBuf,\n                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                 const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t z0 = (tile / tl.nyb) * tl.zSlab;\n    const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + tl.zSlab, nz);\n    const size_t y0 = (tile % tl.nyb) * tl.yBlock;\n    const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + tl.yBlock, ny);\n    if (z0 >= z1 || y0 >= y1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n\n    // mu rows needed: [rlo, rhi], planes needed: [plo, phi] (inclusive, clamped)\n    const size_t rlo = (y0 > 0) ? y0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t rhi = (y1 < ny) ? y1 : ny - 1;\n    const size_t plo = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t winRows = rhi - rlo + 1;\n    const size_t slotSize = winRows * nx;\n\n    auto slot = [&](const size_t z) { return muBuf + (z % 3) * slotSize; };\n    auto row = [&](const double* base, const size_t y, const size_t z) { return base + z * plane + y * nx; };\n\n    size_t next = plo;  // next mu plane to compute\n    for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? tile < tl.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / tl.nyb) * tl.zSlab;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + tl.zSlab, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % tl.nyb) * tl.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + tl.yBlock, ny);\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                    cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\na=s.index('    // Allocate arrays ('); {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfgkVZk3wufFWVnxTDWtk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've parallelized the code with OpenMP. On the 2-socket, 256-thread EPYC 7763 here it runs about 80× faster than the original at 512³, and the output is bit-for-bit the same. I changed only `cahn_hilliard.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`, and the executable is still `cahn_hilliard`.\n\n**Correctness:** the `-r` output (sum, min, max, samples, hash) matches the original exactly. I checked 256³ and nine unusual grid shapes, including 1×1×1, single-row/plane cases, sizes that don't divide evenly and 0 iterations, each at 1, 3 and 256 threads. The build gives no warnings.\n\n**Performance** (with no OpenMP environment variables set):\n\n| Grid, 20 steps | Original | Parallel |\n|---|---|---|\n| 256³ | 2010 ms (≈167 M cells/s) | ≈25 ms |\n| 512³ | ≈128 M cells/s | ≈10,000 M cells/s |\n\n**What changed:**\n- **CMake:** `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linking to `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, so OpenMP is always used.\n- **Merged kernels:** the chemical potential and update steps now run as one pass. Each thread works on its own block of the grid and keeps the three chemical-potential planes it needs in a small (~256 KB) buffer that stays in cache. That buffer replaces the full-size `mu` array, which no longer round-trips through main memory, and this doubled speed on large grids. Block sizes are chosen so every thread gets an even share of work.\n- **One parallel region** covers the whole time loop, so there is a single synchronisation point per step. The inner x-loops vectorise, with the boundary points handled separately using the same arithmetic as before.\n- **Memory placement across the two sockets:** each array is first written by the thread that will later compute on it, so it sits on that thread's socket.\n- **Thread pinning:** without `OMP_PLACES` or `OMP_PROC_BIND`, threads weren't pinned and moved between cores, which made the program about 3× slower. If neither variable is set, the program now pins threads to CPUs itself using `sched_setaffinity` (part of glibc, not a new dependency). If you set either variable, your settings are used instead.\n- **Validation and the result output** run after the timed section. Each thread works on its own block of the grid and keeps the three chemical-potential planes it needs in a small (~256 KB) buffer that stays in cache. That buffer replaces the full-size `mu` array, which no longer round-trips through main memory, and this doubled speed on large grids. Block sizes are chosen so every thread gets an even share of work.\n- **One parallel region** covers the whole time loop, so there is a single synchronisation point per step. The inner x-loops vectorise, with the boundary points handled separately using the same arithmetic as before.\n- **Memory placement across the two sockets:** each array is first written by the thread that will later compute on it, so it sits on that thread's socket.\n- **Thread pinning:** without `OMP_PLACES` or `OMP_PROC_BIND`, threads weren't pinned and moved between cores, which made the program about 3× slower.
